未來展望                           Prospect
2020年將是更加充滿不確定性的一年 ，為應對        2020 is expected to be a year of uncertainty. To cope with the
2020年的挑戰 ，本集團將作出如下措施：          challenges in 2020, the Group will adopt the following measures:


1.   以市場為導向經營管理                1.   Market-oriented operation and management
     2020年 ，在市場充滿不確定性的環境下 ，         In 2020, amid an uncertain market environment, the Group
     本集團將繼續堅持以市場為導向進行經營管            will continue its market-oriented operation and management
     理 ，把客戶滿意度放在中心位置 ，打造人盯          approach, and will put the emphasis on customer satisfaction,
     客戶 、過程跟蹤 、日銷日清 、人單合一的          creating a distinct marketing culture in which the sales staff give
     特色營銷文化 。為迎合客戶需求 ，本集團           full attention to the customers across the whole sales process,
     還將進行產品升級 ，依照客戶需求進行改善           clear all stocks every day, and align their individual goal with
     生產 ，進一步發展中高端產品 。另外 ，本          the sales target. To cater for customer needs, the Group will also
     集團還將進一步控制銷售流程 ，降低銷售成           undergo project upgrades, improving the production process
     本。                             according to customer needs and further developing middle to
                                    high end products. In addition, the Group will further control the
                                    sales process and lower the cost of sales.


2.   加強研發 ，保證持續發展              2.   Strengthen research and development and ensure
                                    a sustainable development

     認為研發是公司持續發展的基礎 ，因此本集           to independent research and development, and considers it as
     團能夠長期以來在行業中保持較高的競爭優            the foundation for a sustainable development of the Company.
     勢 。2020年 ，本集團將構建科技研發技術總        Therefore, the Group has been able to sustain a relatively high
     監機制 ，統領各分支研發機構 ，推動公司發          competitive advantage in the industry for a long period of time.
     展戰略實施 。本集團還將構建創新的科技研           In 2020, the Group will establish a research and development
     發體系 ，創造一個良好的文化環境和氛圍 ，          technology directing management system which will lead
     創造有力的支持條件 ，建立長效的制度支            various research and development branches and promote the
     持 。本集團將以市場為導向 ，推動研究院及          implementation of the development strategy of the Company.
     各公司新產品 、新應用的落實 ，滿足市場需          The Group will also establish an innovative research and
     求 ，貢獻新增量 、新效益 。                development system, and create a good culture, environment
                                    and ideal conditions for a long-lasting support to the system. The

3.    降低成本以提高競爭力                 3.   Reduce the cost to enhance competitiveness
      在市場產品價格下降的情況下 ，本集團將進            Against a backdrop of product price decline in the market, the
      一步降低成本以提高市場競爭力 。2020年 ，         Group will improve its competitiveness by further lowering the
      本集團在生產方面將以降低物耗 、能耗 、            cost. In 2020, the Group will concentrate reduction on resources
      維修費用和環節費用為重點 ，以降低生產成            and energy consumption, maintenance expenses and procedural
      本 。另外 ，本集團還將進一步優化採購流            costs in terms of production to lower the production cost. In

      的防險 ，推遲外地員工春節復工時間 ，有          in the early stage of the Pandemic, the Group immediately issued

      差 ，並制定嚴格的防疫措施 。同時 ，針對         postponed the resumption of work for non-local employees

      將做好一切準備 ，應對疫情帶來的影響 。          the impact caused by the Pandemic to the Group. Together we

根據本公司組織章程細則第87(1)條 ，於每屆股東   Pursuant to Article 87(1) of the Company’s articles of association, at
週年大會上 ，當時三分之一在任董事（或倘董事      each of the annual general meeting, one third of the Directors for the

東岳集團有限公司（「本公司」）董事會（「董事會」）    The board of Directors (the “Board”) of Dongyue Group Limited (the
肩負為本公司股東（「股東」）創造價值及帶來最大      “Company”) recognizes their mission of creating values and maximizing
回報的使命 ，同時履行其企業責任 。為此 ，本公     returns to the shareholders of the Company (the “Shareholders”), while
司矢志提升及保持最高水準的企業管治 。          at the same time fulfilling their corporate responsibilities. To this end,
                             we strive to promote and uphold the highest standard of corporate
                             governance.

主席與行政總裁的角色                  Roles of chairman and chief executive officer
守則第A.2.1條規定 ，主席與行政總裁的角色應有   Code A.2.1 of the Code stipulates that the roles of chairman and chief
區分 ，且不應由同一人兼任 。張建宏先生目前身     executive officer should be separate and should not be performed
兼本公司主席與行政總裁 。董事會認為 ，由一人     by the same individual. Mr. Zhang Jianhong is both the Chairman
同時兼任主席及行政總裁可令本集團更能貫徹強       and Chief Executive Officer of the Company. The Board believes that
勢領導 ，且在業務策劃上更具效率 。此外 ，董     vesting the roles of both Chairman and Chief Executive Officer in the
事會認為 ，在由多名經驗豐富且具才幹的人士所      same position provides the Group with stronger and more consistent
組成董事會密切監控下 ，此架構不會影響權力平      leadership and allows for more effective planning. Further, the Board
衡 。董事會對張先生充滿信心 ，並深信現時安排     considers that this structure will not impair the balance of power, which
對本集團的業務前景最為有利 。             has been closely monitored by the Board, which comprises experienced
                            and high caliber individuals. The Board has full confidence in Mr. Zhang
                            and believes that the current arrangement is beneficial to the business
                            prospect of the Group.

提名委員會負責 。提名委員會就相關事項（包括      been delegated to the Company’s Nomination Committee, which

議 。董事會認為 ，預期新任董事應具備有關範疇     including the appointment or reappointment of the Directors of the
的專業知識 ，以為本公司作出貢獻 ，且有足夠時     Company. The Board considers that the new Director(s) is expected to

A.   政策聲明                      A.   Policy Statement
     本公司明白並深信建立一個多元的董事會的            The Company recognizes and embraces the benefits of having a
     裨益良多 。一個真正多元的董事會可容入並           diverse Board. A truly diverse Board will include and make good
     善用董事的不同技能 、地區及行業經驗 、背          use of differences in the skills, regional and industry experience,
     景 、種族 、性別及其他資歷 。此等差異將          background, race, gender and other qualities of Directors. These
     用作決定董事會的最佳組合 。董事會所有的           differences will be taken into account in determining the optimum
     委任均以用人唯才為原則 ，並於考慮人選時           composition of the Board. All Board appointments will be based
     以客觀條件充分顧及董事會成員多元化的裨            on meritocracy, and candidates will be considered against
     益。                             objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of diversity of
                                    the Board.


B.   可測量目標                     B.   Measurable Objectives
1.   提名委員會將就實施董事會成員多元化每年       1.   The Nomination Committee will discuss and agree annually
     討論及議定可測量目標 ，並向董事會作出建           measurable objectives for implementing diversity on the Board
     議以供採納 。                        and recommend to the Board for adoption.


2.   本公司旨在建立並維持董事會董事在技能 、      2.   The Company aims to build and maintain a Board with a diversity
     經驗 、知識 、專長 、文化 、獨立身份 、年        of Directors, in terms of skills, experiences, knowledge, expertise,
     齡及性別方面的多元化 。                   culture, independence, age and gender.


C.   監察及匯報                     C.   Monitoring and Reporting

     化角度匯報董事會的組成 ，並監察政策的實           Governance Report, on the Board’s composition under diversified
     施情況 。                          perspectives, and monitor the implementation of the Policy.

董事及外聘核數師各自的責任                       Respective responsibilities of Directors and
                                    external auditors
董事負責編製真實公平反映本集團財務狀況及本               The Directors are responsible for the preparation of the financial statements,
集團業績及現金流量的財務報表 。外聘核數師則              which give a true and fair view of the state of affairs of the Group and of
負責根據審核結果 ，對董事編製的財務報表提出              the Group’s results and cash flows. The external auditors are responsible
獨立意見 ，並僅向本公司股東報告 ，除此之外不             to form an independent opinion, based on the audit, on the financial
可作其他用途 。                            statements prepared by the Directors and report the opinion solely to the
                                    members of the Company as a body and for no other purpose.

ESG管理                               ESG Management

理 ，公司建立了董事會領導 、工作小組組織 、相            efficient ESG management, the Company developed an ESG
關職能部門執行的ESG管理體系 ，明確環境 、社            management system guided by the Board, organized by task groups

超強颱風利奇馬於2019年8月11日在山東省登                                    Super typhoon Lekima made landfall in Shandong Province
陸 ，是2019年以來登陸中國的最強颱風 ，共造                                   on 11 August 2019, which was the strongest typhoon
成中國1402.4萬人受災 。受此次颱風影響 ，8月                                 arriving China since 2019, affecting a total of 14.024 million
10日8時至13日17時 ，山東省16市均出現暴雨以                                 people in China. Affected by the typhoon, 16 cities in
上降水 ，多地降雨量突破歷史極限值 。   「利奇馬」                                Shandong Province experienced precipitation above heavy
所過之處急風驟雨、洪水滔滔、摧屋折樹，                                        rain level between 8 a.m., 10 August and 5 p.m, 13 August,
特別是山東濰坊 、棗莊等地勢低窪之處積水嚴                                      which the record-breaking precipitation was experienced
重 ，造成重大災害 。                                                in various places. The places being swept by Lekima
                                                           experienced severe wind and rain, with flooding that
                                                           caused destruction of houses and trees. In particular, low-
                                                           lying areas such as Weifang and Zaozhuang in Shandong
                                                           had serious water accumulation, resulting in major disasters.

  獨立核數師報告
  INDEPENDENT AUDITOR’S REPORT




致東岳集團有限公司全體股東                To the shareholders of Dongyue Group Limited
（於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公司）             (Incorporated in Cayman Islands with limited liability)


意見                           Opinion
 吾等已審核第125至253頁所載東岳集團有限公司    We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Dongyue
（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「貴集團」）的綜     Group Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (together the
 合財務報表，當中包括於2019年12月31日的綜合   “Group”) set out on pages 125 to 253, which comprise the consolidated
 財務狀況表及截至該日止年度的綜合損益及其他       statement of financial position as at 31 December 2019, and the
 全面收入報表、綜合權益變動表及綜合現金流量       consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive
 表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括主要會計政策       income, the consolidated statement of changes in equity and the
 概要。                         consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then ended, and
                             notes to the consolidated financial statements, including a summary of
                             significant accounting policies.


吾等認為，綜合財務報表已遵照國際會計準則理        In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and
事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈的國際財務報      fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31

 本集團選擇可行權宜方法將國際財務報告          The Group has elected the practical expedient to apply IFRS 16
 準則第16號應用於過往已識別為租賃的合約        to contracts that were previously identified as leases applying IAS
 （採用國際會計準則第17號及國際財務報告        17 and IFRIC-Int 4 Determining whether an Arrangement contains
 詮釋委員會詮釋第4號斷定安排是否包含租         a Lease and not apply this standard to contracts that were not
 賃），且不會將此準則應用於過往已識別為         previously identified as containing a lease. Therefore, the Group
 包含租賃的合約。因此，本集團並無重新評         has not reassessed contracts which already existed prior to the
 估於首次應用日期前已存在的合約。            date of initial application.

 就於2019年1月1日或之後訂立或修訂的合約      For contracts entered into or modified on or after 1 January 2019,
 而言，本集團根據國際財務報告準則第16號        the Group applies the definition of a lease in accordance with the
 所載要求應用租賃的定義，以評估合約是否         requirements set out in IFRS 16 in assessing whether a contract
 包含租賃。                       contains a lease.

      國 際 會 計 準 則第1號 及 國 際 會 計 準 則 第8號      Amendments to IAS 1 and IAS 8 Definition of Material
      （修訂本）重大的定義


      該等修訂本通過在作出重要性判斷時納入額                   The amendments provide refinements to the definition of material
      外的指引及解釋，從而對重大的定義進行了                   by including additional guidance and explanations in making
      改進。尤其是，修訂：                            materiality judgments. In particular, the amendments:


      •    包括「掩蓋」重要資料的概念，其效果與               •      include the concept of “obscuring” material information
           忽略或漏報資料類似；                              in which the effect is similar to omitting or misstating the
                                                   information;


      •    將影響使用者的重要性門檻從「可能影                •      replace threshold for materiality influencing users from “could
           響」替換為「可合理預計將影響」
                         ；及                        influence” to “could reasonably be expected to influence";
                                                   and


      •    包括使用「主要使用者」一詞，而非於決               •      include the use of the phrase “primary users” rather than
           定在財務報表中所披露的資料時過於空                       simply referring to “users” which was considered too broad
           泛地僅提述「使用者」。                             when deciding what information to disclose in the financial
                                                   statements.


      該等修訂亦符合所有國際財務報告準則的定                   The amendments also align the definition across all IFRSs and will
      義，並將在本集團於2020年1月1日開始之年                be mandatorily effective for the Group’s annual period beginning
      度期間強制生效。預期應用該等修訂不會對                   on 1 January 2020. The application of the amendments is not
      本集團的財務狀況及表現產生重大影響，惟                   expected to have significant impact on the financial position and
      可能會影響綜合財務報表的呈列及披露。                    performance of the Group but may affect the presentation and
                                            disclosures in the consolidated financial statements.

 債的公平值時會考慮該等特點。該等綜合財         liability if market participants would take those characteristics into

      此外，出於財務報告目的，公平值計量應基                   In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value
      於公平值計量的輸入值的可觀察程度以及該                   measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the
      等輸入值對整體公平值計量的重要性，被歸                   degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are
      入第一層、第二層或第三層的公平值級次，                   observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value
      詳述如下：                                 measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:


      第一層輸入值是指於計量日期由相同資產或                   Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets
      負債在活躍市場中（未經調整）的報價得出；                  for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the
      第二層輸入值是指由除了第一層輸入值所包                   measurement date; Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted
      含的報價以外的，資產或負債可由直接或間                   prices included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset
      接觀察得出的輸入值；及第三層輸入值是指                   or liability, either directly or indirectly; and Level 3 inputs are
      資產或負債的不可觀察輸入值。                        unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.

      綜合基準                                  Basis of consolidation
      綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司及其附                   The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
      屬公司控制的實體的財務報表。當本公司具                   statements of the Company and entities controlled by the
      備以下條件時，即具有控制權：                        Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the
                                            Company:


      •    有控制被投資方的權力；                      •      has power over the investee;


      •    可或有權自其與被投資方的關係中獲得                •      is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
           不同回報；及                                  involvement with the investee; and


      •    有能力運用其權力影響其回報。                   •      has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.


      倘事實和情況顯示上述的三項控制因素之一                   The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
      項或多項出現變動，本集團會重新評估其是                   facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or
      否控制被投資方。                              more of the three elements of control listed above.

      客戶合約收益                                Revenue from contracts with customers
      本集團於完成履約責任時，即於特定履約責                   The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance
      任相關的貨品或服務的「控制權」轉讓予客戶                  obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or services
      時確認收益。                                underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred to
                                            the customer.


      履約責任指可明確區分的一項貨品或服務                    A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a
      （或一組貨品或服務），或一系列大致相同的                  bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct
      可明確區分的貨品或服務。                          goods or services that are substantially the same.


      倘符合以下其中一項標準，則控制權隨時間                   Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over
      轉移，而收益則參照完全履行相關履約責任                   time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of
      的進展情況而隨時間確認：                          the relevant performance obligation if one of the following criteria
                                            is met:


      •    客戶在本集團履約的同時即取得並消耗                •      the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the
           本集團履約所帶來之利益；                            benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the Group
                                                   performs;


      •    本集團履約導致產生及提升一項資產，                •      the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset that
           而該項資產於產生或提升時由客戶控                        the customer controls as the Group performs; or
           制；或


      •    本集團履約未產生對本集團具有替代用                •      the Group’s performance does not create an asset with
           途之資產，而本集團有執行權收取迄今                       an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an
           已履約部分之款項。                               enforceable right to payment for performance completed to
                                                   date.


      否則，收益於客戶獲得特定貨品或服務的控                   Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the
      制權時確認。                                customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.

      所有可扣減暫時差額確認。若於一項交易                    assets are generally recognised for all deductible temporary

      實際利息法是一種計算金融資產或金融負債                   The effective interest method is a method of calculating the
      的攤銷成本及於有關期間攤分利息收入及利                   amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of
      息開支的方法。實際利率是於初步確認時準                   allocating interest income and interest expense over the relevant
      確地透過金融資產或金融負債預計年期或                    period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts
      （如適用）於較短期間內將估計日後現金收款                  estimated future cash receipts and payments (including all fees
      及付款（包括屬整體實際利率一部分的所有                   and points paid or received that form an integral part of the
      已付或已收費用及利息、交易成本及其他溢                   effective interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or
      價或折讓）貼現為賬面淨值的利率。                      discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset or
                                            financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the
                                            net carrying amount on initial recognition.

 (iv) 撇銷政策                     (iv) Write-off policy
      資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財務困難及無             The Group writes off a financial asset when there is
      實際收回可能時（例如，當對手方處於             information indicating that the counterparty is in severe
      清盤狀態或進入破產程序時）（以較早發            financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of
      生者為準），本集團則撇銷金融資產。             recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been

       經考慮法律意見後（倘合適），遭撇銷的             Financial assets written off may still be subject to
       金融資產可能仍須按本集團收回程序進              enforcement activities under the Group’ s recovery

       項。任何其後收回在損益中確認。                appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition event.
                                      Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.


 (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認            (v)    Measurement and recognition of ECL
       預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概率、違約              The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability
       損失率（即違約損失程度）及違約風險的             of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the
       函數。違約概率及違約損失率乃基於根              loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The
       據前瞻性資料調整的歷史數據評估。預              assessment of the probability of default and loss given
       期信貸虧損的預估乃無偏概率加權平均              default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-
       金額，以發生違約的風險為權重確定。              looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased
                                      and probability-weighted amount that is determined with
                                      the respective risks of default occurring as the weights.


       一般而言，預期信貸虧損按本集團根據              Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual
       合約應收的所有合約現金流量與本集團              cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance with
       預計收取的現金流量的差額估計，並按              the contract and the cash flows that the Group expects to
       初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。                receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined
                                      at initial recognition.

      (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認（續）                   (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL (Continued)
          倘預期信貸虧損按集體基準計量或迎合                     Where ECL is measured on a collective basis or cater for
          個別工具水平證據未必存在的情況，則                     cases where evidence at the individual instrument level may

5. 資本風險及財務風險管理目標                           5. Capital risk and financial risk management

           本集團並無重大集中的信貸風險。銀行                       The Group has no significant concentrations of credit risk.
           結餘及現金、已抵押銀行存款及貿易應                       The carrying amounts of bank balances and cash, pledged
           收款項的賬面金額反映本集團金融資產                       bank deposits and trade receivables represent the Group’s
           的最高信貸風險。於2019年及2018年12                  maximum exposure to credit risk in relation to financial
           月31日，所有銀行結餘均存入國有銀行                      assets. As at 31 December 2019 and 2018, all bank balances
           及信譽良好的金融機構，故無重大信貸                       were deposited in reputable financial institutions and were
           風險。管理層預期不會因該等對手方不                       hence without significant credit risk. Management does
           履約而招致任何損失。信貸銷售僅提供                       not expect any losses from non-performance by these
           予信貸記錄良好的選定客戶。本集團已                       counterparties. Credit sales are made to selected customers
           制訂政策確保及時追收未償還的貿易應                       with good credit history. The Group has policies in place to
           收款項。貿易應收款項須應用預期信貸                       ensure that outstanding trade receivables are collected on
           虧損模式。本集團應用國際財務報告準                       a timely basis. Trade receivables are subject to the expected
           則第9號簡化方法計量預期信貸虧損，                       credit loss model. The Group applies IFRS 9 simplified
           於初步確認時，為所有貿易應收款項撥                       approach to measuring expected credit losses which uses a
           備整個存續期內的預期虧損。為計量預                       lifetime expected loss allowance for all trade receivables from
           期信貸虧損，貿易應收款項已根據應佔                       initial recognition. To measure the expected credit losses,
           信貸風險特點及過期天數分類。預期虧                       trade receivables have been grouped based on shared credit
           損率基於銷售的歷史付款情況及出現的                       risk characteristics and the days past due. The expected loss
           相應過往信貸虧損。過往虧損率作出調                       rates are based on the historical payment profiles of sales
           整以反映影響客戶結算應收賬款能力的                       and the corresponding historical credit losses experienced.
           因素的當前及前瞻性資料。                            The historical loss rates are adjusted to reflect current and
                                                   forward-looking information on factors affecting the ability of
                                                   the customers to settle the receivables.

           銷售已竣工住宅物業之收益乃於特定時                       Revenue from sale of completed residential properties is
           間點確認。此乃合法轉讓完成的時間，                       recognised at a particular point in time. This is the time
           即客戶有能力指示物業的使用並取得物                       when legal assignment is completed which is the time when
           業的絕大部分剩餘利益的時間。在此收                       the customer has ability to direct the use of the property
           益確認點之前從客戶收取的按金及分期                       and obtain substantial all of the remaining benefits of the
           付款乃計入綜合財務狀況表中的「合約                       property. Deposits and instalments received from customers
           負債」。                                    prior to this revenue recognition point are included in
                                                   “Contract liabilities” in the consolidated statement of financial
                                                   position.


      6.2 經營分部                              6.2 Operating Segments
           本集團業務乃按產品及物業開發的類型                       The Group’s operations are organised based on the different
           劃分。向本公司董事會（即總營運決策                       types of products and property development. Information
           人）報告以作資源分配及分部表現評估                       reported to the board of directors of the Company, being the
           的資料亦按產品及物業開發的類型呈                        chief operating decision maker, for the purposes of resource
           列。此乃本集團之構造基礎。                           allocation and assessment of segment performance focuses
                                                   on types of products and property development. This is the
                                                   basis upon which the Group is organised.


           本集團之經營及呈報分部如下：                          The Group’s operating and reportable segments are as
                                                   follows:


           •      製冷劑；                             •    Refrigerants;

                  宅物業。                                  properties at Shandong Province, the PRC.

                  部、高分子材料分部、有機硅分                        products of refrigerants segment, polymers segment,
                  部及二氯甲烷、PVC及燒鹼分部的                      organic silicone segment and dichloromethane, PVC

      於釐定非上市股本投資公平值時，本集團委                          In determining the fair value of unlisted equity investments, the
      聘獨立專業估值師進行有關估值。該金額乃                          Group engages an independent professional valuer to perform
      根據估計未來現金流量貼現至其現值的現金                          such valuation. The amount is determined based on the cash flow

      貼現率、最終增長率、預算收益及毛利率，                          present value and requires the use of key assumptions, including
      並考慮到相關行業增長預測及管理層就對市                          the discount rate, terminal growth rate, budgeted revenue

                                                   growth forecasts and financial budgets approved management’s

25. 貿易及其他應收款項（續）                                      25. Trade and other receivables (Continued)
      在承接任何新客戶前，本集團會調查新客戶                                Before accepting any new customer, the Group conducts research
      的信譽及評估潛在客戶的信貸質素和確定客                                on the creditworthiness of the new customer and assesses the

35. 退休福利計劃                                          35. Retirement benefits plans
      本集團在中國的僱員乃中國各個地方政府營                                The employees of the Group in the PRC are members of state-
      運的國家退休福利計劃的成員。本集團須向                                managed retirement benefit schemes operated by the respective
      計劃供款指定百分比（按薪酬成本計算），作                               local governments in the PRC. The Group is required to contribute
      為有關福利的資金。本集團的唯一責任是對                                a specified percentage of payroll costs to the schemes to fund the
      該等計劃作出指定供款。                                        benefits. The only obligation of the Group with respect to these
                                                         schemes is to make the specified contributions.

39. 報告期後事項                      39. Events after the reporting period
  a.    新型冠狀病毒的影響                    a.    Effect of the coronavirus

        自2020年初以來，冠狀病毒疫情爆發為                The coronavirus outbreak since early 2020 has brought
        本集團的經營環境帶來了額外不確定因                  about additional uncertainties in the Group’s operating
        素，並為本集團的營運、財務狀況及業                  environment and has impacted the Group’s operations and
        績帶來影響。                             financial position and performance.


        本集團一直密切監察疫情發展對本集團                  The Group has been closely monitoring the impact of the
        業務的影響，並已採取應變措施。該等                  developments on the Group’s businesses and has put in
        應變措施包括：加大營銷力度；降低經                  place contingency measures. These contingency measures
        營成本；暫停經營外開支；加快客戶回                  include: step up marketing effort; reducing operation cost;
        款；以及降低融資成本。本集團將隨著                  putting on hold of non-operating expenses; speeding up
        疫情的發展，持續檢討其應變措施。                   payment from customers; and lowering finance cost. The
                                           Group will keep its contingency measures under review as
                                           the situation evolves.


        本集團將密切關注冠狀病毒疫情的發                   The Group will pay close attention to the development of the
        展，並評估對本集團財務狀況及經營業                  coronavirus outbreak and evaluate its impact on the financial
        績的影響。於授權本財務報表發佈當                   position and operating results of the Group. As at the date
        日，本集團尚未發現疫情對財務報表構                  on which this set of financial statements were authorized
        成任何重大不利影響。                         for issue, the Group was not aware of any material adverse
                                           effects on the financial statements as a result of the outbreak.
